--- d106 [the Table] ---

The choice is which risk you take. ◈ the Feared — 36 distinct monsters faced blow-by-blow. The footage exists, and it plays unasked. (standing: 100) ◇ the Indispensable — The Indispensable needs a full seat — the galaxy must actually need you, not just notice you. ◈ the Beloved — Viewership 21, fan favor 34 — the galaxy's favorite, by the numbers. (standing: 72) ◇ the Witness — You didn't keep the list (0 exposures on record — the trial needs 3). ◈ the Assimilated — Always open. The table takes anyone — that's what makes it a trap, and what makes it safe. (standing: 55) ◈ the Defiant — Defiance 9 on the record. The 'no' is earned. (standing: 56) ◈ the Relay — Stage-3 neural integration. The matrix doesn't care about your kingdom — it cares how deep you are. (sysbond 0) (standing: 40)
